Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

Port-after-redesign (later steps): editing primitives (copy/wire/set/delete/create/inline/batch) as VibeWorkflow methods then CLI; visualization/SVG layout; MCP adapter as thin layer over schema/analysis/search/runtime APIs. Do NOT port: raw JSON editing as primary UX; old submit/logs; Claude-skill-files-as-product-structure; duplicated MCP-only analysis. Also do structural cleanup: resolve vendor/direct_templates vs workflow_corpus disconnect (cli.py sources sync defaults are wrong), delete dead top-level input/, delete empty docs/comfyscript_spike/, split monolithic cli.py into vibecomfy/commands/ submodules, dedupe the three runpod scripts via a shared scripts/runpod_runner.py, rename templates/ to ingest/ (or split templates/+normalize/), add module homes vibecomfy/schema/ vibecomfy/analysis/ vibecomfy/search/, and expand the thin README.md. Constraints: VibeWorkflow stays the only editable IR; JSON is input/output only; embedded Comfy is default runtime; preserve runpod_validate.py / runpod_model_matrix.py / runpod_corpus_matrix.py entrypoints; preserve all existing CLI commands.

        Plan:

# Implementation Plan: Port useful old VibeComfy capabilities into the new package

## Overview
The new `vibecomfy` package is centered on `VibeWorkflow` as the only editable IR; JSON is input/output only and embedded Comfy is the default runtime. This plan ships two tracks together:

1. **Structural cleanup** so the port has a clean home: split monolithic `vibecomfy/cli.py` into `vibecomfy/commands/`, dedupe the three RunPod scripts behind `scripts/runpod_runner.py`, fix wrong `sources sync` defaults that point at non-existent `vendor/workflow_templates`, kill dead/empty paths (`input/`, `docs/comfyscript_spike/`), split `vibecomfy/templates/` (five modules: loader/normalize/index/library/**ready**) into `vibecomfy/ingest/` (loader + normalize + index) and `vibecomfy/registry/` (library + **ready**), fix the latent template-id resolver bug (`workflow_from_template` ignores the external index whenever the official one exists), and add module homes `vibecomfy/schema/`, `vibecomfy/analysis/`, `vibecomfy/search/`.
2. **Port-early features**: schema-aware validation backed by `/object_info` (reusing the existing `ValidationIssue`/`ValidationReport` types), graph analysis (`analyze`/`trace`/`upstream`/`downstream`/`path`/`subgraph`/`values`/`diff`/`unconnected`) on `VibeWorkflow`, and node search with weighted scoring + task aliases (`i2v`/`t2v`/`t2i`/`controlnet`/`wan`/`ltx`/`audio_reactive`/`flux`/`sdxl`/`qwen`/`hunyuan`/`inpaint`/`outpaint`).

Port-after-redesign work (editing primitives, SVG/visualization, MCP adapter) is **out of scope** — follow-up plan.

Repository touch points (with current evidence — re-verified iteration 3):
- `vibecomfy/cli.py:190-192` — `sources sync` defaults `vendor/workflow_templates` / `vendor/external_workflows` (non-existent); real corpus is `workflow_corpus/{official,custom_nodes,manifests}`.
- `vibecomfy/cli.py:14` imports `ready_template_ids, workflow_from_ready` from `vibecomfy.templates.ready`. `cli.py:32-35` (`workflows list --ready`), `cli.py:122-124` (`run --ready`), `cli.py:199` (`workflows list`'s `--ready` flag), `cli.py:228` (`run`'s `--ready` flag).
- `vibecomfy/templates/` has **five** modules — `loader.py`, `normalize.py`, `index.py`, `library.py`, **`ready.py`** — all must move in lockstep.
- `vibecomfy/templates/ready.py:9` reads from `READY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "ready_templates"` — i.e. `<repo>/ready_templates/`. After rename to `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`, `parents[2]` still resolves to the same repo root, so `READY_ROOT` does **not** need to change.
- `vibecomfy/templates/library.py:17-21` — current resolver: if `template_index.json` exists, never consults `external_workflow_index.json`. Latent bug.
- `vibecomfy/templates/normalize.py:60-63` — `widget_<i>` keys are emitted **inside `normalize_to_api()`**, before `convert_to_vibe_format()` runs. Direct callers of `normalize_to_api()` (e.g. the three `runpod_corpus_matrix.py` heredocs at 155-156, 168-169, 181-182) bypass `convert_to_vibe_format` and dump API JSON directly. Schema hook must therefore land on `normalize_to_api`, not on `convert_to_vibe_format`.
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:120-129` — `validate()` only checks structural; returns `ValidationReport` with `ValidationIssue` (must be reused, not re-defined).
- `vibecomfy/workflow.py:120` callers: `vibecomfy/cli.py` (validate, doctor, run/inspect via `.ok`), `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:28`, `tests/test_workflow_core.py:22`, `tests/test_scratchpad_loader.py`.
- `tests/test_workflow_core.py:6-7` imports from `vibecomfy.templates.{index,normalize}`.
- `tests/test_ready_templates.py` imports from `vibecomfy.templates.ready`.
- `scripts/runpod_corpus_matrix.py:155-156, 168-169, 181-182` — embedded Python heredocs `from vibecomfy.templates.{loader,normalize}` — must update lockstep.
- `input/` (top-level) is empty/unused; `docs/comfyscript_spike/` is empty (content lives in adjacent `.md`).

Constraints:
- VibeWorkflow stays the only editable IR; analysis is read-only.
- JSON is input/output only — no new "edit JSON" CLI surface.
- Embedded runtime stays the default for `vibecomfy run`.
- All existing CLI subcommands keep their names, args (including `--ready`, `--limit`, `--backend`, `--prompt`, `--seed`, `--steps`, `--runtime`, `--server-url`, `--mode`, `--tail`, `--official`, `--external`, `--custom-nodes`, `--out`), exit codes, and stdout shape.
- The three RunPod entrypoint script names stay the same.

## Phase 1: Structural cleanup (mechanical, no behavior change)

### Step 1: Delete dead paths and fix `sources sync` defaults (`vibecomfy/cli.py`, `input/`, `docs/comfyscript_spike/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Delete** top-level `input/` directory and its empty `3d/` child.
2. **Delete** empty `docs/comfyscript_spike/` directory (keep adjacent `docs/comfyscript_spike.md`).
3. **Fix** `vibecomfy/cli.py:190-192` defaults so `vibecomfy sources sync` with no flags works against the local corpus:
   - `--official` default → `workflow_corpus/official`
   - `--external` default → `workflow_corpus/custom_nodes`
   - `--custom-nodes` default stays `custom_nodes`.
4. **Update** `runpod_validate.py:20` `EXCLUDE_DIRS` entry `"vendor/workflow_templates"` → `"vendor/direct_templates"`.
5. **Update** `runpod_model_matrix.py:22-32` `OFFICIAL_CANDIDATES` paths from `vendor/workflow_templates/templates/...` to `workflow_corpus/official/...` equivalents.

### Step 2: Split monolithic `cli.py` into `vibecomfy/commands/` (`vibecomfy/cli.py`, new `vibecomfy/commands/`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py` exposing each command module's `register(subparsers)`.
2. **Move** every `_cmd_*` handler + its `add_parser` block out of `vibecomfy/cli.py`:
   - `commands/sources.py` (sync), `commands/workflows.py` (list — **preserve `--ready` flag** wired to `ready_template_ids()`), `commands/nodes.py` (list), `commands/inspect.py`, `commands/convert.py`, `commands/validate.py`, `commands/doctor.py`, `commands/run.py` (**preserve `--ready` flag** wired to `workflow_from_ready()`), `commands/runtime.py` (doctor + smoke), `commands/logs.py`.
3. **Reduce** `vibecomfy/cli.py` to: parser builder that walks registered command modules, `main()`, and `_resolve_workflow_path()` helper (CLI-only consumers).
4. **Confirm** `pyproject.toml:24` script entry `vibecomfy = "vibecomfy.cli:main"` still works.
5. **Run** `python -m pytest -q tests` (covers `test_ready_templates.py`, `test_workflow_core.py`, `test_scratchpad_loader.py`).
6. **CLI smoke**: `vibecomfy workflows list --ready` and (without launching a pod) `vibecomfy run --ready <template_id> --runtime embedded` argument-parse cleanly (use `--help` if a smoke run isn't available locally).

### Step 3: Split `vibecomfy/templates/` (five modules) into `vibecomfy/ingest/` + `vibecomfy/registry/` (lockstep across all callers)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Move** all five files:
   - `vibecomfy/templates/loader.py` → `vibecomfy/ingest/loader.py`
   - `vibecomfy/templates/normalize.py` → `vibecomfy/ingest/normalize.py`
   - `vibecomfy/templates/index.py` → `vibecomfy/ingest/index.py`
   - `vibecomfy/templates/library.py` → `vibecomfy/registry/library.py`
   - **`vibecomfy/templates/ready.py`** → **`vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`** (ready-template lookup is registry-shaped — same job as `library.py`).
2. **Verify** `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py:9` `READY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "ready_templates"` still resolves to `<repo>/ready_templates/` after the move (`vibecomfy/registry/ready.py` → `parents[2]` = repo root). No change needed; just sanity-check by reading the resolved path in a quick `python -c`.
3. **Add** `vibecomfy/ingest/__init__.py` re-exporting `load_template`, `detect_workflow_shape`, `normalize_to_api`, `convert_to_vibe_format`, `index_workflows`, `write_index`.
4. **Add** `vibecomfy/registry/__init__.py` re-exporting `workflow_from_file`, `workflow_from_template`, **`ready_template_ids`**, **`workflow_from_ready`**.
5. **Update every caller in lockstep** (no shim, clean break — full call set):
   - `vibecomfy/__init__.py` re-exports.
   - `vibecomfy/scratchpad_loader.py` (uses `vibecomfy.workflow_from_*` — verify still works after the `vibecomfy/__init__.py` re-exports point at registry/).
   - `vibecomfy/commands/workflows.py` and `commands/run.py` (the `--ready` callers from Step 2): `from vibecomfy.registry.ready import ready_template_ids, workflow_from_ready`.
   - `vibecomfy/commands/inspect.py`, `commands/convert.py`, `commands/sources.py`: `from vibecomfy.ingest.{loader,normalize,index} …`.
   - `tests/test_workflow_core.py:6-7`: `vibecomfy.templates.{index,normalize}` → `vibecomfy.ingest.{index,normalize}`.
   - **`tests/test_ready_templates.py`**: `vibecomfy.templates.ready` → `vibecomfy.registry.ready`.
   - **`scripts/runpod_corpus_matrix.py:155-156, 168-169, 181-182`** — three embedded Python heredocs: `from vibecomfy.templates.loader` → `from vibecomfy.ingest.loader`; `from vibecomfy.templates.normalize` → `from vibecomfy.ingest.normalize`.
   - `scripts/runpod_validate.py` and `scripts/runpod_model_matrix.py` (no `vibecomfy.templates` imports today; verify with `Grep` after the move).
6. **Remove** the old `vibecomfy/templates/` directory entirely.
7. **Verify** `Grep -r "vibecomfy\.templates"` returns zero hits across the repo (package, tests, scripts, docs).
8. **Run** `pytest -q tests` and `python -m vibecomfy.cli sources sync` against `workflow_corpus/`.

### Step 4: Fix `workflow_from_template` to merge both index files (`vibecomfy/registry/library.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Today's bug** at `vibecomfy/templates/library.py:17-21` (post-rename `vibecomfy/registry/library.py`): if `template_index.json` exists, the resolver **never** consults `external_workflow_index.json`. `sources sync` writes both. `vibecomfy/cli.py:_resolve_workflow_path` already iterates both indexes (correct shape).
2. **Change** `workflow_from_template(template_id)` to read both `template_index.json` and `external_workflow_index.json` (whichever exist), concatenate, then look up `template_id`. Match by `id` first, then by `Path(path).stem`. Raise `KeyError` only when neither index has it.
3. **Add a regression test** in `tests/test_workflow_core.py` (or a new `tests/test_registry.py`): write both index files to `tmp_path` (CWD-swapped), register an id only in `external_workflow_index.json`, assert `workflow_from_template` resolves it.

### Step 5: Dedupe RunPod scripts behind `scripts/runpod_runner.py` (`scripts/runpod_runner.py`, three runpod entrypoints)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `scripts/runpod_runner.py` exposing:
   - `class PodGuard` (current shape — `launch()` / `terminate()` / `_start_watchdog()` / `_terminate_after()`), parameterized on `name_prefix` and `max_runtime_seconds_env`.
   - `should_skip(path, root, exclude_set)` and `upload_dir(sftp, local, remote, exclude_set)`.
   - `install_signal_handlers(loop)`.
   - `async def run_pod(remote_script: str, *, name_prefix: str, exclude: set[str], upload_mode: Literal["sftp_walk", "tarball"] = "sftp_walk", timeout: int) -> int` orchestrating launch → wait → upload → exec → terminate.
2. **Rewrite** each `runpod_*.py` to import shared helpers and define only its `EXCLUDE_DIRS`, remote bash script string, and `main()`. Keep file paths and entrypoint names identical.
3. **Verify** `python -c "import scripts.runpod_validate"` (and the other two) succeeds before any pod call.
4. **Do not** auto-launch pods.

### Step 6: Expand `README.md`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** the 12-line stub. Cover: project goal, the new module layout (`vibecomfy/{workflow,ingest,registry,runtime,schema,analysis,search,commands}`), CLI subcommand list with one-line descriptions (**including `--ready` flag for `workflows list` and `run`**), the `sources sync` → `workflows list` → `inspect` → `convert` → `validate` → `analyze info` → `search` → `run` quick start against `workflow_corpus/`, and links to `docs/old_vibecomfy_port_rationale.md` and `docs/vibeworkflow.md`.
2. **Sweep** `docs/*.md` for references to `vibecomfy.templates` or `vibecomfy/templates/`. Today only `docs/old_vibecomfy_port_rationale.md:76` mentions templates conceptually (no import-path string) — re-grep after Step 3 to confirm.

## Phase 2: Schema-aware validation (`vibecomfy/schema/`)

### Step 7: Add a schema provider that talks to `/object_info` (`vibecomfy/schema/__init__.py`, `vibecomfy/schema/provider.py`, `vibecomfy/schema/cache.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Define** `NodeSchema` dataclass — `class_type: str`, `pack: str | None`, `inputs: dict[str, InputSpec]` (`type`, `required`, `default`, `choices`, `min`, `max`), `outputs: list[OutputSpec]` (`type`, `name`).
2. **Implement** `RuntimeSchemaProvider`:
   - Check existing `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py` for an `/object_info` helper; if absent, add a thin `httpx.AsyncClient.get("/object_info")` call there.
   - Reuse `vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:comfy_server` to start an embedded server on demand and cache aggressively under `out/cache/object_info.<runtime_fingerprint>.json`. Externally-running server URLs are also accepted.
3. **Implement** `LocalSchemaProvider` — falls back to `node_index.json` (already produced by `sources sync`) when no runtime is reachable; provides `class_type`, `pack`, and a coarse input list.
4. **Expose** `get_schema_provider(prefer="runtime"|"local"|"auto")` factory.

### Step 8: Hook schema-aware widget mapping at the `normalize_to_api()` layer (`vibecomfy/ingest/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Small-Medium
1. **Today** `vibecomfy/ingest/normalize.py:60-63` writes positional `widget_<i>` keys **inside `normalize_to_api()`**, before any `convert_to_vibe_format()` call. Direct `normalize_to_api()` callers (e.g. `runpod_corpus_matrix.py` heredocs) bypass `convert_to_vibe_format` entirely.
2. **Add** an optional kwarg `normalize_to_api(raw, *, schema_provider=None) -> dict`:
   - When `schema_provider` is `None` and the `comfy.component_model.workflow_convert` import succeeds, behavior is **identical to today** (existing tests untouched).
   - When `schema_provider` is provided and the fallback path runs, map each positional `widgets_values[i]` to the declared input name from `NodeSchema.inputs` (in declared order) instead of writing `widget_<i>`. Skip any input already populated by an incoming edge (don't overwrite a connection with a positional widget value). Fall back to `widget_<i>` only when the schema lookup yields nothing for that `class_type`.
3. **Thread** the kwarg through `convert_to_vibe_format(api_workflow, *, source_path=None, workflow_id=None, schema_provider=None)`: it accepts `schema_provider`, passes it to `normalize_to_api` if it ever calls normalize internally, but the primary effect is that callers who construct the workflow via `convert_to_vibe_format(normalize_to_api(raw, schema_provider=p), ...)` use the same provider once.
4. **Update callers** that should use schema-aware mapping:
   - `commands/inspect.py`, `commands/convert.py`, `commands/run.py`, `commands/validate.py`, `commands/doctor.py` — when constructing the API dict, pass `schema_provider=get_schema_provider("auto")` to `normalize_to_api`. (validate/doctor already construct the provider for Step 9; share it.)
   - `vibecomfy/registry/library.py:workflow_from_file` and `workflow_from_template` accept `schema_provider=None` kwarg and thread it down.
   - **Do not** change `runpod_corpus_matrix.py` heredocs to pass a provider — they run on a remote pod where the schema may not be available; the kwarg defaults to `None`, so the embedded calls keep working unchanged. The shape change just means *if* a provider were available there, the existing call site is the right one.
5. **Existing tests** (`tests/test_workflow_core.py:test_ui_workflow_normalizes_to_api`, etc.) call `normalize_to_api(raw)` with no kwargs — unchanged behavior, tests pass.

### Step 9: Wire schema-backed validation into `VibeWorkflow.validate()` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`, `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py`, per-caller)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Reuse** the existing `vibecomfy.workflow.ValidationIssue` and `ValidationReport` types. Issue codes added: `unknown_class_type`, `missing_required_input`, `unknown_input`, `type_mismatch`.
2. **Add** `vibecomfy/schema/validate.py:validate_against_schema(workflow, provider) -> list[ValidationIssue]` (importing `ValidationIssue` from `vibecomfy.workflow`):
   - `class_type` exists in schema → `unknown_class_type` (error).
   - Required inputs present in `node.inputs ∪ node.widgets ∪ <incoming edges>` → `missing_required_input` (error).
   - No unknown keys in `node.inputs ∪ node.widgets` → `unknown_input` (error).
   - Edge type compatibility → `type_mismatch` (warning; custom nodes lie about types).
3. **Extend** `VibeWorkflow.validate()` signature in `vibecomfy/workflow.py:120` to `validate(self, schema_provider=None) -> ValidationReport`. When `schema_provider is None`, behavior is identical to today.
4. **Per-caller wiring**:
   - `commands/validate.py` — `get_schema_provider("auto")`, pass it.
   - `commands/doctor.py` — same.
   - `commands/inspect.py:_cmd_inspect` — already calls `workflow.validate().ok` to print runnable status; pass schema provider so the status reflects schema validity (preserving existing stdout shape `status: runnable|unsupported`).
   - `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:28` — **structural-only**. One-line code comment to record intent. Run path must not pay schema lookup cost on every invocation.
   - `tests/test_workflow_core.py`, `tests/test_scratchpad_loader.py`, `tests/test_ready_templates.py` — pass nothing.

### Step 10: Tests for schema validation (`tests/test_schema.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cover** with a fake `SchemaProvider`:
   - Unknown `class_type` → one `ValidationIssue(code="unknown_class_type", severity="error")`.
   - Missing required input → one issue with input name in message.
   - Unknown input → one issue.
   - Type-mismatched edge → one issue with `severity="warning"`.
   - `isinstance(issue, vibecomfy.workflow.ValidationIssue)` for every issue (parallel-type regression guard).
   - With `schema_provider=None`, `validate()` matches today's structural-only behavior on the `tests/test_workflow_core.py` fixture.
2. **Cover** `normalize_to_api(..., schema_provider=p)`:
   - With a schema that declares `["text", "clip", "ckpt_name"]` for a `class_type`, a UI workflow with three positional widgets emits keys `text`, `clip`, `ckpt_name` instead of `widget_0`, `widget_1`, `widget_2`.
   - With `schema_provider=None`, output matches today's `widget_<i>` shape (regression guard).

## Phase 3: Graph analysis module (`vibecomfy/analysis/`)

### Step 11: Build the analysis primitives on `VibeWorkflow` (`vibecomfy/analysis/graph.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** as pure functions taking `VibeWorkflow`, no I/O:
   - `analyze(workflow) -> dict` — node count, edge count, fan-in/fan-out histogram, output sinks, terminal inputs, detected media type.
   - `trace(workflow, node_id) -> list[VibeNode]` — full upstream chain.
   - `upstream(workflow, node_id, depth=None) -> set[str]`.
   - `downstream(workflow, node_id, depth=None) -> set[str]`.
   - `path(workflow, src, dst) -> list[list[str]]` — all simple paths, capped at depth 32.
   - `subgraph(workflow, node_ids) -> VibeWorkflow` — new VibeWorkflow with only given nodes/edges; never mutates source.
   - `values(workflow, node_id=None) -> dict` — literal `inputs ∪ widgets`, skipping linked inputs.
   - `diff(workflow_a, workflow_b) -> dict` — added/removed/changed nodes, edge add/remove sets, input value changes.
   - `unconnected(workflow, schema_provider=None) -> list[dict]` — required inputs not satisfied. With schema, uses `NodeSchema.inputs[*].required`; without, reports nodes with no incoming edges and no widget defaults for non-source nodes.
2. **Reuse** existing `VibeWorkflow`, `VibeNode`, `VibeEdge`, `VibeOutput` types — no parallel shapes.

### Step 12: Expose analysis as CLI subcommands (`vibecomfy/commands/analyze.py`)
**Scope:** Small-Medium
1. **Add** parent subcommand `vibecomfy analyze` with sub-subcommands `info`, `trace`, `upstream`, `downstream`, `path`, `subgraph`, `values`, `diff`, `unconnected`.
2. **Each** sub-subcommand accepts a workflow positional resolved by `_resolve_workflow_path` (correctly walks both indexes after Step 4). Verb-specific args: `--node-id`, `--src`, `--dst`, `--max-depth`, `--format json|tsv|text`.
3. **Default** human-readable; `--format json` machine-readable.
4. **Wire** via `commands/__init__.py`.

### Step 13: Tests for analysis (`tests/test_analysis.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Build** a small synthetic `VibeWorkflow`; assert each function returns expected sets/lists. Cover `diff` with a small mutation. Cover `subgraph` non-mutation by re-asserting source workflow equality.
2. **CLI smoke**: `subprocess.run([..., "vibecomfy", "analyze", "info", "<fixture>.json"])` returns 0 with non-empty stdout.

## Phase 4: Node search (`vibecomfy/search/`)

### Step 14: Search bootstrap — guarantee indexes exist before search runs (`vibecomfy/search/bootstrap.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/search.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Today** `node_index.json` is written by `sources sync`; `external_workflow_index.json` includes custom-node example data. Neither is guaranteed on a fresh checkout.
2. **Add** `vibecomfy/search/bootstrap.py:ensure_indexes(*, auto_sync: bool = False) -> None`:
   - Checks for `node_index.json`, `template_index.json`, `external_workflow_index.json`.
   - If any missing and `auto_sync=False` → raise `SearchBootstrapError("indexes missing; run 'vibecomfy sources sync' first")`.
   - If `auto_sync=True` → run the same code path `vibecomfy sources sync` runs (factor a shared helper out of `commands/sources.py:_cmd_sources_sync` and call it from both).
3. **Default** for `vibecomfy search`: `ensure_indexes(auto_sync=False)`; surface bootstrap error with exit code 1. Add `--auto-sync` flag.

### Step 15: Build the search index and weighted scorer (`vibecomfy/search/index.py`, `vibecomfy/search/aliases.py`, `vibecomfy/search/scorer.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Define** `SearchEntry` — `class_type`, `pack`, `description`, `tags`, `tasks`, `source: Literal["object_info","node_index","custom_node_examples","curated"]`, `path: str | None`.
2. **Build the corpus** by merging (after `ensure_indexes`):
   - Runtime schema provider (Step 7) — full input/output type info per `class_type`.
   - `node_index.json`.
   - Custom-node example workflows by reusing `vibecomfy/nodes/index.py:index_custom_node_examples`.
   - Curated metadata from `workflow_corpus/manifests/coverage.json` — `task` + `model_family` + `media`.
3. **Add** `vibecomfy/search/aliases.py` task-alias map: `i2v`, `t2v`, `t2i`, `controlnet`, `wan`, `ltx`, `audio_reactive`, `flux`, `sdxl`, `qwen`, `hunyuan`, `inpaint`, `outpaint`.
4. **Implement** weighted scorer: exact `class_type` (5) > pack (3) > tag (2) > description token (1); alias hits +2 each (stack). Top-N descending.

### Step 16: Add CLI commands `vibecomfy search` and `vibecomfy nodes spec` (`vibecomfy/commands/search.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py`)
**Scope:** Small-Medium
1. **`vibecomfy search <query>`** — supports `--task <alias>`, `--limit`, `--auto-sync`. Calls `ensure_indexes` (Step 14), prints id / class_type / pack / score / source.
2. **Extend** `vibecomfy nodes` (today: only `list`) with `nodes spec <class_type>` — print full `NodeSchema`.
3. **Wire** into the parser builder.

### Step 17: Tests for search (`tests/test_search.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Fixture** with in-memory `SearchEntry` list; assert task aliases produce expected hits, exact `class_type` wins over partial, pack-match ranks above description-only.
2. **Bootstrap test**: with no index files in `tmp_path` (CWD swapped), assert `ensure_indexes(auto_sync=False)` raises and the CLI surfaces a clear error pointing at `vibecomfy sources sync`.
3. **Smoke**: `vibecomfy search wan --task i2v` against the local `workflow_corpus/` returns ≥1 entry (after `sources sync`).

## Phase 5: Validation rollup

### Step 18: Run the existing test suite + manual CLI smokes
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `python -m pytest -q tests` — `test_workflow_core.py`, `test_scratchpad_loader.py`, `test_ready_templates.py`, plus the new `test_schema.py`, `test_analysis.py`, `test_search.py` (and any new `test_registry.py`) all pass.
2. **CLI quick-start** against the local corpus:
   ```bash
   python -m vibecomfy.cli sources sync
   python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list --limit 5
   python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list --ready --limit 5
   python -m vibecomfy.cli inspect z_image
   python -m vibecomfy.cli analyze info workflow_corpus/official/image/z_image.json
   python -m vibecomfy.cli search wan --task i2v
   python -m vibecomfy.cli validate examples/smoke_empty_image_red.json
   ```
3. **Confirm** `python -c "import scripts.runpod_validate"` (and the other two) succeeds.

### Step 19: Confirm RunPod entrypoints still launch (info-only)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Document** in PR description that `runpod_validate.py` / `runpod_model_matrix.py` / `runpod_corpus_matrix.py` were not actually run (cost). Step 3 updated the embedded heredocs and Step 5 dedup'd the launcher; user re-runs after merge.

## Execution Order
1. Phase 1 first — structural cleanup unblocks new module homes.
2. Within Phase 1: 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 → 6. Step 3's rename happens before Step 4's library fix (which lives in the new file location). The Step 2 `--ready` flag preservation is verified by `tests/test_ready_templates.py` continuing to pass; Step 3 makes the ready import path move work.
3. Phase 2 (schema) before Phase 3 and Phase 4. Step 8 (normalize hook) must land before `commands/inspect.py` etc. start passing schema providers down.
4. Phase 3 (analysis) and Phase 4 (search) serialize 3 → 4. Search depends on Step 4's resolver fix and on Phase 2's schema provider.
5. Phase 5 last.

## Validation Order
1. After each Phase 1 step, run `pytest -q tests` + targeted CLI smokes (sources sync against new defaults; `workflows list --ready` + `run --ready --help`; `Grep -r "vibecomfy\.templates"` zero hits after Step 3).
2. After Steps 9, 11, 14–16, run the new module's targeted tests first, then full `pytest`.
3. After Phase 5, run the manual CLI quick-start from Step 18.
4. RunPod scripts: import-only check; do not auto-run pods.

      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",

        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-143",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-145",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-142",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the supporting-infrastructure review still finds one missing check. in `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md`, sprint 2's verification matrix and risk register both single out `self_refiner` as a distinct drift-upgrade surface with its own smoke and its own silent-behavior-change risk, but the revised v4 body updates the file without keeping that supporting verification step in the actual execution checklist.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-146",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-143",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

  {
    "subsystem": "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements",
    "total_occurrences": 3,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },
  {
    "subsystem": "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure",
    "total_occurrences": 3,
    "plan_count": 3,
    "entries": [
